%fused_computation (param_0.4: f64[8,8], param_1.4: f64[8,8]) -> f64[2,4,5,1,9] {
  %param_0.4 = f64[8,8]{1,0} parameter(0)
  %param_1.4 = f64[8,8]{1,0} parameter(1)
  %sub.0 = f64[8,8]{1,0} subtract(%param_0.4, %param_1.4), metadata={op_name="jit(boundary_force_program)/sub" stack_frame_id=620}
  %slice.3 = f64[8,5]{1,0} slice(%sub.0), slice={[0:8], [0:5]}, metadata={op_name="jit(boundary_force_program)/slice" stack_frame_id=888}
  %transpose.1716 = f64[5,8]{0,1} transpose(%slice.3), dimensions={1,0}, metadata={op_name="jit(boundary_force_program)/transpose" stack_frame_id=889}
